It exists so that the headless reference sortie (Acceptance Criteria) and the Game Feel arc can name "where in the sortie" without minting state. *(orchestrator ruling: the stage ruling R12 says "no Boarding state names"; the task requires this table; the resolution is to keep the table as test-fixture vocabulary, state this once, and let the labels be dropped or renamed at the checkpoint without touching any rule. Logged as a question.)*

**The four owner axes the labels read** (nothing else is consulted):

| Axis | Owner field | Values used |
|---|---|---|
| Where | `body_of_actor` / `actor_section` (BDY); `transit_state` (WM) | home body; derelict locus body; in transit (`IDLE`, `LEG`, `ARRIVING`, `BERTHING`) |
| What the hands do | `controller.state.loco_state` (PC) and `channel_ref` | `IDLE`, `WALK`, `RUN`, `CARRY_WALK`, `CHANNELLING`, `STAGGERED`, `DOWNED`, `IN_POD` |
| What is held | `controller.state.carry_ref` (PC-8), cross-checked with `suit.state.carried_id` (SE-13) | none / one carried instance |
| What the web is doing | Attention `phase` (AT) | `CALM`, `TELL`, `BREACH`, `SUBSIDING` |

**Labels (derived, never stored):**

| Label | Derived from | Entry | Exit | Legal transitions | Who may cause them |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| HOME | actor in the home body; `transit_state` `IDLE` | boot, or arrival from a hop | `pod_hop` Applied | to TRANSIT_OUT | the player (`pod_hop`, WM-8) |
| TRANSIT_OUT | `transit_state` `LEG` with origin home; `loco_state` `IN_POD` | `pod_hop` Applied (a body transfer; aborts any channel, IA-40) | `pod_arrive` / `pod_settle` after `HOP_LEG_TICKS` and `ARRIVE_SETTLE_TICKS` | to ABOARD_QUIET | Web Map's machine (WM-14); the player only by pressing the console |
| ABOARD_QUIET | actor in a derelict locus body; no `channel_ref`; hands free or a tool in hand; `phase` not `TELL`/`BREACH` | arrival; a channel ends or aborts; `drop` | a channel begins; `pickup`; a TELL opens | to ABOARD_JOB, LOADED, ABOARD_TELL | the player (verbs); Attention (phase) |
| ABOARD_JOB | `loco_state` `CHANNELLING` on `begin_cut`, `begin_cap` or `field_check` | channel `Applied` (lit, in reach) | completion; abort (IA-15: MOVED, CANCELLED, UNLIT, OUT_OF_REACH, TARGET_GONE, BODY, NOT_ELIGIBLE, RESTORE); a TELL opens (the channel is not aborted by it) | to ABOARD_QUIET, ABOARD_TELL | the player; the abort set; Attention (phase) |
| LOADED | `carry_ref` set; `loco_state` `CARRY_WALK` or `IDLE` | `pickup` Applied (IA-28) | `drop`; downed (PC-9); `install_module`; `pod_hop` carries it across (state persists) | to ABOARD_QUIET (drop), HOME (via hop), INSTALLED | the player; Suit's limiter via PC-9 (downed) |
| ABOARD_TELL | `phase` `TELL` and the actor in the locus body | Attention enters TELL (AT-8) | abort by stillness (AT-10), breach due, or departure | to ABOARD_QUIET (abort), ABOARD_BREACH, TRANSIT_BACK | Attention only; the player's restraint or departure shapes it |
| ABOARD_BREACH | `phase` `BREACH` or `SUBSIDING`, actor in `locus_body` (AT-15) | tell expiry | the last recorded occupant leaves a non-home locus body (AT-22) | to TRANSIT_BACK | Attention (the harm), the player (departure) |
| TRANSIT_BACK | `transit_state` `LEG` with origin a derelict; `loco_state` `IN_POD` | `pod_hop` Applied from a dock room (always allowed, WM-26) | arrival at home | to HOME (and still LOADED if `carry_ref` is set) | the player; Web Map's machine |
| INSTALLED | M5 `install_module` Applied (MSA-26) | the module seated in a hardpoint | **terminal for that module** (no `remove_module`, MSA-28; V8 salvage is the only way out) | none | the player (`install_module`) |
| DOWNED | `loco_state` `DOWNED` (Suit limiter FAILED, SE-24) | the limiter reaches FAILED | revive (VS-B); `DOWN_TO_DEATH_TICKS` elapses, then DEAD, then restore from the newest autosave (SL-7) | to ABOARD_QUIET (revive), restore | Suit/Controller (the limiter); the host (restore) |

**Transition rules worth stating once.**

1. **LOADED is an overlay, not a place.** It combines with every aboard label (a tell during a carry is ABOARD_TELL and LOADED at once); the fixture reports both.
2. **Any aboard label may go to TRANSIT_BACK from any dock room with the pod docked there, whatever the cargo** (WM-26, WM:414 envelope). If the console row is refused (C-BS-1), a LOADED actor must first `drop` and the module stays aboard (Edge Cases).
3. **A tell does not interrupt a hop, and a hop does not interrupt a tell** (AT-22, WM-26); a departed derelict goes dormant and its state stands still (WM-24, LS-17).
4. **Restore returns each axis to its owner's snapshot** and the labels are recomputed: `CHANNELLING` and `STAGGERED` go to `IDLE`, `carry_ref` is restored with the Suit's instance and cross-checked (PC-17, SE-13 `SUIT_RESTORE_CARRY_MISMATCH`); Interaction's `post_restore` never touches `carry_ref` (IA-28); a cut that completed before the autosave stands, one that did not is as if never begun (the ledger is Interaction's).
5. **Nothing here is guarded by Boarding.** Every transition is caused by a verb, a hop or an Attention phase that an owner validates; Boarding adds no guard, no timeout and no side effect to any of them.

*Why.* A named, registered sortie machine would restate `loco_state`, the Attention phase and `transit_state` (run ruling R12). *Pillar.* P3 (the cost is in the steps, not in a state). *Decided.* run ruling R12 (the table kept as fixture vocabulary).

### Interactions with Other Systems

Boarding & Salvage owns **no interface** (BS-1): no hook, no mutator, no read view, no command, no notification, no dirty key. "Data in" below is what the sortie reads from an owner; "data out" is what the sortie supplies — which is always a rule, a content constraint, a procedure or a routed ask, never runtime data. Every name in the interface column is fixed by its owner (the kernel, the Graph, the Controller, Interaction, Suit, Assembly, Sanity, Web Map, Save/Load); where an owner has not fixed it, the cell says so. **Each row quotes the neighbour's own counterpart row so bidirectionality can be checked line by line.** Status: **Designed** unless marked *provisional*.
