The Attention `phase` axis is read only by the DIAGNOSTIC test fixture. [BS-2]

### C. Enter, listen, light, decide

- **AC-BS-10 (auto)** GIVEN an actor idle for 600 ticks aboard with the beam off and the home terms excluded WHEN the Attention intake is read THEN the noise pushed is 0 and the level `L` is non-increasing toward `B`; AND the Input Map and the command vocabulary contain no action or command named `listen`; AND, when a TELL is open, the structure-borne tell bed's gain on the bus is identical between a moving and a held-still actor (read from the bus, not asserted as a number). [BS-4]
- **AC-BS-82 (auto)** GIVEN the closed list of consequence-channel witnesses in LSG:44 WHEN the `scr_a` falsifier fixture is built THEN no assertion in the `scr_a` falsifier depends on HUM_DROP alone; a HUM_DROP raised by the cut of a running machine is read as true-state audio (LSG:976). [BS-4, BS-17]
- **AC-BS-11a (auto)** GIVEN a target that is not lit WHEN `begin_cut`, `begin_cap`, `field_check`, `pickup` or `install_module` is pressed THEN each is refused with the owner's named reason (`NOT_LIT` for the TAPs and the channel press; the exact word per verb as the neighbour defines it) and 0 tool ticks accrue. [BS-5]
- **AC-BS-11b (auto)** GIVEN a channel in progress WHEN the target goes dark mid-channel THEN it aborts with cause `UNLIT` and the ledger keeps the partial. [BS-5]
- **AC-BS-11c (auto)** GIVEN everything dark THEN `drop` and `toggle_beam` work, and `throw_glowstick` needs no lit target. [BS-5]
- **AC-BS-83 (auto)** GIVEN a thrown glowstick WHEN it is in flight THEN the target is unlit; WHEN it lands THEN it is lit, never before; WHEN the actor carries a module THEN `throw_glowstick` is `BUSY`; WHEN a lit `begin_cut` runs THEN the emitter exposure ledger holds an entry for the job; WHEN a module is dropped in the dark and a stick is thrown THEN the `pickup` is accepted; a ninth live deployable follows Emitter's `MAX_DEPLOYABLES_LIVE` 8 rule (the observed behaviour is recorded). [BS-5]
- **AC-BS-12a (lint)** GIVEN every Boarding-owned or cited sprite brief, prompt and card THEN no value tag, rating, condition bar, rarity mark or loot-glow token appears. [BS-6]
- **AC-BS-12b (auto)** GIVEN `inspect` on a loose item or on a module at the MVP THEN no card is produced; GIVEN `inspect` on a lit instrument THEN the card is In-World Instruments' (live at MVP, IW-21) and carries no value tag, rating or condition bar. [BS-6]
- **AC-BS-12c (manual)** GIVEN a tester at the decide step THEN the evidence they cite comes from the world (the module's sprite, its stub, the closed-list witnesses, what home lacks) and not from a tag; folded into the session of AC-BS-70 to AC-BS-78. [BS-6]

### D. The cut and the cap

- **AC-BS-13a (auto)** GIVEN `begin_cut` held WHEN it is aborted before completion THEN the module remains in place and the ledger keeps the partial; the module remains until completion. [BS-7]
- **AC-BS-13b (auto)** GIVEN `begin_cut` held to completion THEN the yield is one LOOSE_WORLD item per yielded instance at the node anchor and **never auto-carried**: `controller.state.carry_ref` and `suit.state.carried_id` are both empty immediately after completion. [BS-7]
- **AC-BS-13c (auto)** GIVEN a cut of a def with fluid ports THEN one STUB is registered per `open_end` fluid port, equal to the def's port count in IDB-7. [BS-7]
- **AC-BS-14a (auto, snapshot of `REF_GOLDEN`)** GIVEN the **D.2 golden schedule** in order (walk-in 312 ticks at 1 AU, door attempts 60 idle, the cut 240 ticks at `U_TOOL` with `U_LOUD` on the last tick, the pickup dwell 12 idle, the carry 624 ticks at 1 AU, the console approach 30 idle) from `L0 = B` 300,000 mAU, noise only, read out of the running integrator WHEN the sortie ends THEN the peak is exactly **1,756,600 mAU** at the end of the carry and the level after the console is **1,732,482 mAU**; AND, as the tolerance-proof assertion across schedule variants, the peak share of `ATT_THRESHOLD` is within **28.5 to 30.0 percent**, the Attention `phase` stays CALM, and the peak occurs at the end of the carry. (Omitting the 12-tick pickup dwell gives 1,762,817 mAU, inside the band.) [D.2]
- **AC-BS-14b (auto, snapshot of `REF_GOLDEN`)** GIVEN the same schedule with the beam (288) and home (766) terms from `L0` 1,678,501 mAU WHEN it ends THEN the peak is **3,394,795 mAU** and the level after the console **3,374,965 mAU** (57 percent of `ATT_THRESHOLD`), and the Attention `phase` equals the phase `REF_GOLDEN` records (CALM at the current placeholders; the OQ-BS-27 ruling regenerates the file and the crossing assertions of AC-BS-123 replace the CALM expectation)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)***. [D.2]

### I. The dark dock, content constraints and the clocks

- **AC-BS-51a (auto, BLOCKING for implementation when the OQ-BS-4 ruling is M1 or M3; blocked on that ruling)** GIVEN the build at the ruled mechanism (M1, the lit exemption for `pod_hop` on the console; or M3, the reserved lamp charge) and a dock with the beam off, `CELL_S` [PLACEHOLDER] spent and zero sticks WHEN the player presses the console, with or without a module in the arms, THEN `pod_hop` is accepted (M1: at an unlit console; M3: using the reserved charge with the beam otherwise off), the reach test is unchanged (out of reach is still `OUT_OF_RANGE`), and under M1 `berth` and `unberth` stay lit-gated *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. After the ruling the unused one of AC-BS-51a and AC-BS-51b is deleted. [BS-19]
- **AC-BS-51b (lint plus auto, BLOCKING for implementation when the OQ-BS-4 ruling is M2 or another content-side mechanism; blocked on that ruling)** GIVEN a content-side mechanism ruled in OQ-BS-4 (M2, a pod-owned console lamp) WHEN the content lint runs THEN every derelict dock and the home airlock have the console lit by a source that needs neither the player's cell nor a lit pickup, the lint is a build error and not a warning, and the AC-BS-92 matrix passes in that build *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. **The earlier wording is withdrawn:** one authored loose glowstick per dock (`DOCK_CONSOLE_DARK_NO_STICK` as the fallback) cannot close the strand, because a loose stick is unlit until thrown and `pickup` is lit-only, and Web Map declined the strengthened form (ASK-BS-W3). There is no sign-off path: a soft-lock is not an acceptable shipping state. [BS-19]
- **AC-BS-92 (auto, BLOCKING for implementation; a bounded reachability test)** GIVEN the matrix (beam cell in {spent, remaining}) x (sticks in {0, 1, 2 or more}) x (console lit in {yes, no}) x (module in {carried, dropped, none}) **plus the campaign-length strand cell**, that is a prepared save with the cell at 0, sticks at 0, the module in the arms and a dark dock, and the same state reached by *play* from a fresh start through the scripted path of about four lit survey sorties of 18,000 lit ticks each (D.10) with the A-route sticks spent WHEN each cell is explored by a breadth-first search over the verbs {move, drop, pickup, throw_glowstick, toggle_beam, pod_hop} to a fixed depth N (a harness constant above the longest legal sequence, drop, throw, pickup, move, pod_hop, and recorded in the header of `REF_GOLDEN`) THEN `pod_hop` is Applied within N verbs in every cell, and a cell the search cannot resolve within N is a failure and not a skip; the one cell that fails today (cell spent, 0 sticks, unlit console, with or without the module) is the BS-19 strand and **must pass under the ruled mechanism** (AC-BS-51a or AC-BS-51b), and a build in which it fails is not implementation-complete *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. [BS-19, D.10]

- **AC-BS-113 (auto)** GIVEN `SALVAGE_CHANNEL_TICKS` at 180 and 480 and `PARTIAL_TTL_TICKS` at 180 and 1200 THEN the BS-7 claim "a cut begun at tell entry completes inside any tell a player meets" holds at both ends (480 < `TELL_DRAW_LO` 900) and the AC-BS-86 boundary moves with the knob. [BS-7, Tuning Knobs]
- **AC-BS-114 (auto)** GIVEN `CELL_S` 600 and 2400, `HOP_FUEL_ML` 100 and 2000, and `ABORT_HOLD_TICKS` 60 and 300 THEN the D.10, D.6 and D.3 outputs recompute from the owners' live values and no invariant in this group fails. [Tuning Knobs, D.3, D.6, D.10]
- **AC-BS-111 (advisory, performance)** GIVEN the reference sortie in the worst loaded set S4 THEN the logged per-tick step time has no `STEP_BUDGET_MS` 4 overrun attributable to the Boarding window, sampled at 3 runs and reported, not asserted (wall-clock timing is non-deterministic). [BS-1]

### J. Autosave and restore

- **AC-BS-57a (auto; unblocked, Save/Load SL-4 consumes `last_salvage_T()`)** GIVEN a V8 completion at least `AUTOSAVE_MIN_GAP_TICKS` 1800 after the last generation (cut at 10,000, last generation at 7600, so `t_eligible` = 10,000) WHEN Save/Load polls Interaction's salvage-completion derived read `last_salvage_T()` (Boarding mints none) with the gate open and the actor `CARRY_WALK` THEN a generation is written at `t_save` = the first `AUTOSAVE_RETRY_TICKS` 300 boundary of the fixture's poll clock at or after 10,000, asserted as `t_save` equal to that computed boundary and `t_save - 10,000 < 300`; and GIVEN the actor DOWNED or DEAD at the boundary THEN no generation is written *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. [BS-21, D.9]
- **AC-BS-57b (auto; unblocked as above)** GIVEN a cut 600 ticks after a generation THEN `t_eligible` is the generation tick plus 1800, the save waits exactly until the first poll boundary at or after it (1200 ticks of gap, plus at most 299 of poll phase); GIVEN a cap in progress (CHANNELLING) at that boundary THEN the save defers to the next boundary. [BS-21, D.9] *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*
- **AC-BS-58a (auto)** GIVEN a tell with under 600 ticks left THEN a due save defers. [BS-21]
- **AC-BS-58b (auto)** GIVEN a CHANNELLING, STAGGERED or IN_POD seat THEN a due save defers. [BS-21]
- **AC-BS-58c (auto)** GIVEN an absent Attention view THEN a due save defers and logs `GATE_INPUT_ABSENT`. [BS-21]
- **AC-BS-58d (auto)** GIVEN a cut's SEVERED descriptors (EXOGENOUS) THEN they open no row (a) window. [BS-21]
- **AC-BS-58e (auto)** GIVEN a save deferred 7200 ticks THEN it is written `RISKY`; GIVEN a death within `DEATH_LOOP_WINDOW_TICKS` 3600 of restoring a RISKY generation THEN the restore steps back one. [BS-21]
- **AC-BS-59 (auto)** GIVEN a restore mid-carry THEN `carry_ref` equals `suit.carried_id`, a channel in progress is dropped, a cut completed before the autosave stands, and one that did not is as if never begun. [BS-21]
- **AC-BS-60 (auto)** GIVEN a restore in `IN_POD` THEN the leg is restored by Web Map's rules and the module is in the arms on arrival. [BS-21, BS-12]
- **AC-BS-117 (auto)** GIVEN a death after an autosave that followed the cut THEN the restore has the kerf, the stub and the module; GIVEN a death before it THEN the node is intact and the module is on its pedestal (only world state is asserted; the player's knowledge is out of scope for a state test). [BS-21]

[BS-22]
- **AC-BS-62 (visual, review plus import lint)** GIVEN the carried-module sprite THEN one plain shape on the tool layer serves every carried module; the pose is **overhead**, 24 x 14 px at most (AD-BS-1, AD-BS-2; C-BS-5 closed); R-BS-1 and R-BS-2 pass at all five facings (the chest point `|x| <= 2` and the first 12 px of the beam ray clear; zero foot slip at 60 px/s); and figure plus module is at most 71 px. [BS-22]
- **AC-BS-97 (UI)** GIVEN the carry state THEN a retained screenshot in `production/qa/evidence/boarding/` shows the prompt set: `G . Drop`, plus the console and compatible-hardpoint prompts when facing them, and nothing else; no weight number, no value tag. [BS-9, BS-11]
- **AC-BS-98 (UI)** GIVEN the linear channel bar during a cut, a cap and a SCAN THEN a retained screenshot of each shows the bar by Interaction's grammar, with the module still on the deck during the cut. [BS-7, BS-8]
- **AC-BS-99 (visual, screenshot and art-director sign-off)** GIVEN the open-end piece, the cap piece, the kerf and the carried-module sprite THEN a retained screenshot of each in a lit room is on file; the palette lint passes (no flesh or violet); and the carry frame keeps the chest point and the forward beam arc clear. [BS-22]
- **AC-BS-100 (manual)** GIVEN the Visual/Audio section THEN each Boarding-relevant event (stub hiss, pickup, set-down, pod hush, load breath cue) has an event id, a trigger condition and a sync rule from the audio existence list, and a playtester can hear it. [BS-22, Game Feel]

### L. Refusals and cross-references

- **AC-BS-64 (lint)** GIVEN the final GDD WHEN the stale-token lint runs with word-boundary matching (`spawn`) THEN it flags `inventory`, `pack capacity`, `weight limit`, `encumbrance`, `loot table`, `rarity`, `value tag`, `hand point` as a beam origin, `torch`, `auto-carry`, `spawn` for derelict A, `progress ring`, `EXTRACTION_DONE`, and any hook or command name outside AC-BS-3's generated list. **Pass** = zero hits outside a committed whitelist file of line-anchored exceptions, each with its reason; **fail** = any hit outside it. The whitelist file covers BS-24's refusal table, the tone "Avoid" list, the Reference-techniques Reject column, negation sentences in BS-1, BS-6, BS-11 and BS-12, the routed-thread quote in BS-12 and C-BS-2, SE-12 quotes and quoted counterpart or refresh rows; the whitelist itself is reviewed under AC-BS-65, so the lint run needs no reviewer pass and is deterministic. The lint's own regexes are first proved on a seeded-defect fixture (a file containing `inventory`, `pack capacity` and `auto-carry`) that it must flag *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. [BS-24]
- **AC-BS-65 (review)** GIVEN BS-24 THEN guard rails G-1 to G-11 are all present and no rule anywhere implies a refused item. [BS-24]
- **AC-BS-66 (auto)** GIVEN every def in the Item DB, every verb in Interaction's table and every Suit tool def THEN none carries a damage field or a kill verb; `begin_cut`'s legal subject classes are exactly SALVAGE_MODULE and STUB; the cutter's channel never targets an ACTOR; and harm needs a lit `begin_harm` channel that Interaction admits only while Sanity's `actor_sanity_below_harm_threshold` is true for the attacker (a sane actor's attempt is `NOT_ELIGIBLE`; VS-B, stubbed at MVP). [BS-24]
- **AC-BS-67 (review)** GIVEN the refresh table THEN every row has owner, file:line, current words and proposed words, the line cites were re-grepped at authoring (SN:521, SN:959, SN:1432, HE:376, HE:928), rows 17 to 20 are present, AC-SN-107 and AC-SN-108 appear as deferred mirrors (AC-BS-43 to AC-BS-46), and row 9 states that Boarding reads no Attention state. [Cross-References]
- **AC-BS-68 (review)** GIVEN the conflict register THEN C-BS-1 to C-BS-15 each carry a kind, a state in this GDD and a route; C-BS-5 reads ruled; C-BS-9 reads "GAP — the expected end of a long lit session, and a true soft-lock" and the strand is nowhere described as uncommon; C-BS-10 "GAP (low, VS-A)"; C-BS-11 states `T_home` as Sanity defines it; C-BS-14 is reduced *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. [Cross-References]
- **AC-BS-118 (auto, deferred: VS-B)** GIVEN two seats WHEN both press `begin_cut` on the same node THEN the second is `BUSY`; GIVEN a crewmate DOWNED while carrying THEN their `carry_ref` is cleared and the module lands at their foot; no verb takes a module from a crewmate's hands; and GIVEN a sane attacker (`harm_eligible` false) WHEN `begin_harm` is pressed on a crewmate THEN it is `NOT_ELIGIBLE` and nothing changes, and for an eligible attacker the victim's `tell_on` has been true for at least `SAN_TELL_LEAD_S` before and the event is attributed to the web. [BS-7, BS-24]

### M. Playtest targets (manual; five testers, at least three outside the project; thresholds [PROVISIONAL: creative-director])

- **AC-BS-69 (manual)** X-BS-1: first sortie to A, median W_ABOARD (`a_dock` arrival to the console press, D.1) between 3 and 5 min [PROVISIONAL: creative-director] (the upper bound is 5 min and not 6: the median must stay below the sanity stage-1 point, 315.8 s, D.8; an 8 min median would equal `T_SORTIE_SAFE_TICKS`); no tester completes W_LOOP (home console press to install) under 90 s; the observer also records the share of W_LOOP spent in the pod, which is reported against the 40 percent ceiling of the Tuning Knobs pacing row and is not a pass line *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. [D.1, D.8]
- **AC-BS-70 (manual)** X-BS-2: at least 3 of 5 pause 2 s or more, or voice a cost, immediately before their first cut. [BS-7]
- **AC-BS-71 (manual)** X-BS-3 (the `scr_a` falsifier, both paths): of testers who reach `scr_a`, at least 3 of 5 either leave it uncut or, if they cut it, can say afterwards why it was dead from something they saw or heard aboard (a SCAN, a lit lantern elsewhere after the bus is woken, a draught, the room), not "the game told me"; run once with the bus dead and once after the spare breaker is swapped. If it fails, the beat is a trap and the fallback is the content route (a vacant scrubber hardpoint or a placed kit). [BS-17]
- **AC-BS-72 (manual, blocked on the OQ-BS-27 ruling; open no story)** X-BS-4: every tester who meets a tell while carrying knows the module can be set down (5 of 5), and at least 2 of 5 drop it or voice the trade-off; under option A of OQ-BS-27 the row is rewritten to the cut-into-a-rising-tell decision *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. [BS-13]
- **AC-BS-73 (manual)** X-BS-5: asked "how much could you carry?", at least 4 of 5 answer in body or hands terms and none with a number. [BS-11]
- **AC-BS-74 (manual)** X-BS-6: after cutting a fluid-port module, at least 4 of 5 attribute the stub's hiss to their own cut without prompting. [BS-8]
- **AC-BS-75 (manual)** X-BS-7: no more than 1 of 5 calls the carry home boring or tedious unprompted, and at least 3 of 5 call it tense or exposed. [BS-9]
- **AC-BS-76 (manual)** X-BS-8: at least 4 of 5 can point at the installed heater at home and say where it came from. [BS-18]
- **AC-BS-77 (manual, blocked on OQ-BS-27; conditional on OQ-BS-3)** X-BS-9: at least 3 of 5 voice a noise or cost trade-off before a second cut in the same room, which tests whether a single cut is a weigh; withdrawn if the content route of OQ-BS-3 is not ruled, because a second cut then pays nothing *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. [D.2]
- **AC-BS-122 (manual, blocked on the OQ-BS-27 ruling; a precondition of the MVP playtest)** X-BS-11: GIVEN the reference sortie (the cut and carry-home of `htr_a`) played with the beam on and normal dwell by five testers, at least three outside the project, WHEN the observer reads Attention's `phase` from the debug overlay for each sortie from arrival to the console press THEN at least 3 of 5 sorties meet at least one TELL and no more than 4 of 5 do. The row cannot be run, and the MVP kill/continue playtest does not run, until the ruling of OQ-BS-27 has been applied and AC-BS-123 passes on the regenerated `REF_GOLDEN`; a build whose lit golden schedule still peaks below the threshold, or whose tell is not caused by the take, fails this precondition *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. [D.2, BS-7]
- **AC-BS-78 (manual; a precondition of the MVP playtest; blocked on the OQ-BS-4 ruling)** X-BS-10: GIVEN testers started from the **prepared save** (cell 0, sticks 0, the module in the arms, a dark dock) and, separately, from the played path of about four lit survey sorties, WHEN they are asked to go home THEN every tester makes the console press succeed (an observer-recorded Applied `pod_hop`) within 120 s of the start and none reports "I couldn't get home"; which tests the dark-dock invariant, and replaces the chance-encounter form of the row *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*. [BS-19]
- **AC-BS-124 (manual)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* X-BS-12: of testers who meet a closed door with the module in their arms (the home `door_cargo_s`, or a door they shut), at least 3 of 5 find the drop, open, re-take cycle without being told within 60 s of meeting the door; the observer records whether they cite the return of the door's own prompt on the `drop` frame. [BS-10]
- **AC-BS-125 (manual)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* X-BS-13: of testers who reach `door_a_spine_machine`, at least 4 of 5 say, before or as they open it, that the next room is dangerously cold, citing the frost fan over the sill, the visor fog or the breath and not a number or a prompt; at least 3 of 5 shut the door behind them or cross once and leave rather than linger. [BS-17, D.4]
- **AC-BS-126 (manual)** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* X-BS-14: the observer records which door each tester opens first at the `a_spine` junction; at least 2 of 5 reach `a_machine` without being told; the row is a finding for the leading-elements and sightline pass with Derelict Authoring and never a reason to add a marker or a hint (G-5, G-7). [BS-17]

AC-BS-26 and AC-BS-121, which applied only if ASK-BS-I1 was refused, are deleted (ASK-BS-I1 is answered) *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*.
- **Deferred to Vertical Slice (blockers named):** AC-BS-43, AC-BS-44, AC-BS-46 (junk defs IDB-22, the Perception Filter, SN-21), AC-BS-45 (SE RK-2), AC-BS-110 (the Perception Filter), AC-BS-118 (Co-op VS-B), AC-BS-38 (an authored Horror encounter). No class (d) behaviour ships in MVP (BS-15).
- **Depends on Web Map's [PLACEHOLDER] values:** AC-BS-32, AC-BS-108, AC-BS-81, AC-BS-91, AC-BS-93.

These are covered by the manual rows in group M.
- **Not yet fixed by the owners and so asserted loosely:** the exact refusal word per verb in AC-BS-11a is each neighbour's own; the behaviour above `INTERACT_FIXTURE_BUDGET` is Interaction's to state (AC-BS-112).
- **AC-BS-14b and AC-BS-80:** the beam-and-home expectation depends on whether the lit home term keeps feeding the level while aboard; until AC-BS-80 is answered the number is conditional on the assumption stated in D.2.

## Open Questions

Owner is who decides or supplies the answer: **user** (a design decision for the person who owns the game), **agent** (a neighbouring system's designer or a specialist, via the ask in Cross-References), or **GDD** (an answer this document must record when a spike or a lint returns). Target is where the answer will arrive: **playtest**, **next GDD** (the neighbour's authoring or revision) or **ADR** (an architecture decision). **The user-decision items come first.** OQ-BS-2 and OQ-BS-4 keep the numbers BS-10 and BS-19 already cite.

| # | Question | Owner | Target |
|---|---|---|---|
| OQ-BS-1 | **Pod capacity as a number (option B).** The routed thread "pod capacity and weight limit the haul" is met structurally at MVP (BS-12, C-BS-2): one carried module per seat, stack caps, the body cue. Does the Vertical Slice add a **cargo cradle with a class-point ceiling** (a visible physical surface loaded by hand; any refusal a world consequence such as the pod visibly not sealing, never a text reason naming mass)? Pre-listed asks if yes: a Class B transfer on `loose[]`, one `pod_hop` reason, two registry names, `P <= PLAYER_DROP_RESERVE`; risk: the Item DB reserves a pod scale for VS (IDB-21) and the oracle rule (IA-11) must still hold. Revisit at the kill/continue playtest | user | playtest |
| OQ-BS-2 | **Admit V4 `set_opening` while carrying?** BS-10 solves a closed door with drop, open, re-take (about 30 ticks plus two TAPs) and adds no fourth carve-out. Admitting the door press while carrying would remove the cycle at the cost of a larger carry-admitted set (BS-9) and a weaker "doors are the carry's terrain". Recommended: no. Revisit if X-BS-7 reports the cycle as tedious | user | playtest |
| OQ-BS-3 | **USER DECISION — the MVP's second payoff, and where `scr_a` goes.** `scr_a` (FAILED, 40 kg) has no MVP destination (no vacant scrubber hardpoint, no `part_machine_kit`, no `remove_module`); BS-17 keeps it as the honest "the loot was garbage" beat and `htr_a` as the reference payoff. *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* The review's finding is that this is a content gap and not only a playtest risk: the MVP install payoff happens once and the "decide" step is scripted (one fit), a correct read of `scr_a` changes nothing, and sortie 2 onward pays nothing. **Recommended: commit the content route now, not after a failed playtest** — a vacant scrubber hardpoint at home, ideally with two or three competing hardpoints so that "which module, where" is a decision, routed to Modular Ship Assembly and Derelict Authoring. If that is out of scope, the Overview already states that the MVP loop pays off once, the `scr_a` beat is flavour, and X-BS-9 and the strip-the-wreck tests are withdrawn. The falsifier condition stays (X-BS-3 / AC-BS-71: if the beat fails it is a trap and the content route is the fallback). Also the choice between keeping the beat and rewording X-DA-8 (C-BS-6, recommended: keep plus reword) | **user** | before the Vertical Slice content plan; playtest otherwise |
| OQ-BS-4 | **USER DECISION — the dark-dock strand: one ruling in two parts (BS-19, C-BS-9, D.10).** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* Cell spent, zero sticks and an unlit console is a true soft-lock because the dock is survivable, and it is the **expected end of a long lit session** (about 4 survey sorties spend the cell, the MVP has no refill, sticks are finite). Interaction **refused** the lit exemption (IA-6 and Open Question 31: it needs the user's ruling that the stranding guarantee outranks the lit-only rule, P3, and a PC-6 amendment by the Controller owner); Web Map declined the stronger lint (OQ-WM-38 (a)); the fallback `DOCK_CONSOLE_DARK_NO_STICK` cannot close the strand as worded. **(a) Mechanism, pick one:** **M1** a lit exemption for `pod_hop` on the CONSOLE only, the reach test unchanged (recommended: cheapest, no fixture cost, keeps the visible-unknown lint intact; the console must not become a self-lit prop); **M2** a pod-owned console lamp that sits off the derelict bus and is costed in D.2; **M3** a reserved lamp charge that only the console press can spend. Rejected: allowing `throw_glowstick` while carrying, because it removes the BS-5 cost of lighting the drop zone first. **(b) Cell policy, pick one:** promote V7 `top_up` into the MVP (the Graph's LS-26 amendment, Graph Open Question 20), add a home-airlock recharge, or keep the cell non-renewable on purpose (then the mechanism alone holds the invariant). **Once ruled:** rewrite BS-19, C-BS-9, D.10 and this row to the chosen pair; delete the unused one of AC-BS-51a and AC-BS-51b; Web Map updates WM-8, E-16, AC-WM-10 and AC-WM-88, which still read the exemption as provisional; AC-BS-92 and AC-BS-78 are already written to hold for any pair. If the user instead accepts the strand, that changes Pillar 5's failure contract and is not a Boarding waiver. Owners: Interaction (ASK-BS-I3) and the Controller (M1), Emitter State and Web Map (M2), Suit (M3 and the policy), the Graph (V7) | **user** | before implementation of this system (blocking) |
| OQ-BS-27 | **USER DECISION — the anchor's tell decision (Player Fantasy steps 6 and 7; D.2, D.3; X-BS-4, X-BS-11).** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* The decision "carry it home under rising Attention, then keep walking or drop it" cannot happen as written, and retuning one constant will not fix it: there is **no crossing** (57 percent lit, 65 percent at the corner of the safe ranges), **no live choice** (the carry fits every played tell with 36 to 276 ticks to spare), it is **not caused by the take** (28 percent of the threshold is home idle, so lifting `W_L`, `W_P` or `B_table` fires tells the player cannot blame on their cut) and there is **a free third option** (freezing after the cut costs about 0.94 percent of sanity). Choose one, owners Boarding, Attention and Derelict Authoring: **(A) move the decision** — the tell opens at or just after the cut's LOUD report and the decision becomes "cut into a rising tell or not"; the anchor and X-BS-4 are rewritten to match; **(B)** Attention adds a carry or sortie-burden intake term of about 7,000 mAU per tick while carrying (not a mass term, so IDB-21 holds) and the decision-bearing carry is lengthened to about 21 to 28 tiles (DA allows 28) so `fits_carry` is false for the lower part of `TELL_DRAW`; **(C)** the reference decision sortie is a longer B route or a second A node. Whichever is chosen: require causality (the cut plus the carry supply at least about 50 percent of the headroom above `L0`); name freeze as an explicit option, priced or intentionally free; and accept by the deterministic margins on `REF_GOLDEN` (AC-BS-123: the lit run crosses with at least 5 percent margin; the beam-off, home-lamps-off and short-dwell variants stay below `theta_HOLD`). Do not open stories for BS-13 or the tell rows, and do not run the MVP kill/continue playtest, until a retuned D.2 shows a tell that is live and caused by the take. Attention replaces its OQ-AT-22 with its own acceptance for the chosen option | **user** | before the MVP playtest (blocking) |
| OQ-BS-28 | **USER DECISION (light) — the first-sortie time target (BS-20, X-BS-1, D.1, D.8).** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)* The windows are now defined (W_LOOP and W_ABOARD, D.1) and the X-BS-1 band is capped below Sanity's stage-1 point, but nothing in the content produces the time: the aboard floor is 20.6 s and the survey allowance 50.6 s, against a median W_ABOARD target of about 259 s. Choose: (a) keep the concept's five-minute W_LOOP and budget the content for it (witnesses per room, decision points; Derelict Authoring), or (b) re-baseline the target to about 120 to 180 s of W_ABOARD (about 2.7 to 3.7 min of W_LOOP), which departs from the concept's "about five minutes"; and (c) whether entering sanity stage 1 on a long sortie is intended (the band assumes not) | **user** | with the content plan; playtest |

| OQ-BS-10 | **Does the lit reference sortie meet a tell, and is a cut a weigh, at the Attention placeholders (ASK-BS-A1, C-BS-13)?** A cut is about 19 percent of `ATT_THRESHOLD` and decays on a 30 s constant; the noise term of the reference sortie peaks at 29 percent and the full lit push (beam and lit idle home ship, D.2 restated) at 57 percent, so no tell opens on the sortie, and no in-range value of `U_TOOL`, `U_LOUD` and `TAU_ATT_TICKS` alone changes that. **Answering this is a precondition of the MVP playtest** *(Amended 2026-10-27 — design review)*: the review shows a constant-level review is not enough (the carry decision is dead, a tell would not be the take's own doing, and freezing is free), so the ruling is carried by **OQ-BS-27**; this row keeps the intake and light and power review of ASK-BS-A1 for X-BS-9 | agent | with OQ-BS-27 (blocks the MVP playtest) |
